{SARUMAN_RING_AI_BODY}For a long time the White Wizard has been looking for a Great Ring once belonging to the Dark Lord Sauron. {MORDOR_RING_AI_TITLE}Sauron's Servants bring him the ring!
{SARUMAN_MANYCOLOURS_DIES_BODY}The sun rises to join its place in a sapphire sky as the realms of the Free Peoples rejoice. Just this morn, news came from the Orthanc. Even the Elves, within their trees and hidden glades, cannot help but rejoice, sing a song of praise to Elbereth as the news spreads across the land of Middle-earth: Saruman of Many Colours is dead!\n\nThe Maia had come as a guide, sent by the Valar away in the heavenly lands of Valinor, choosing to forsake the easy existence in Aman, to be stripped of his power, and to be sent to the war-torn regions of Middle-earth alongside four others. Their goal was simple: to guide the Free Peoples to the final and utter defeat of the Dark Lord. Saruman the White was the greatest among these, Curunír, the Man of Skill, he was known, and his voice was soothing like a lover’s embrace, sweet as honey. Yet the good will would not last. While the guides sent from Valinor forming the Order of the Istari were stripped of their power in order to not be tempted to fall awry in their task, Saruman the White kept a secret envy in his black heart. This envy of one of his own order grew, until he would prove that he was the greatest of the Istari, that he could be Lord. After all, why should he merely guide when he was born to lead?\n\nSaruman delved into the arts of Ring-crafting, becoming obsessed with the One Ring forged by the Dark Lord in the fires of Orodruin. This preoccupation led Saruman down a dark road, culminating in his theft of the creation of a hideous breed of Sauron’s greatest warriors, the Black Uruks. With his own servants, Saruman declared himself a rival to the Great Eye, coming like a storm upon Rohan, easily sweeping away the resistance of the peaceful folk of the plains. Yet at the last, Saruman’s obsessions had paid off: he had discovered the One Ring to Rule Them All. At last, the greatest of all artefacts, the Ring above all Rings of Power, was his. With it, he bent even the Black Riders to his will, stealing them from Mordor’s service and into the command of a new Dark Lord; the Iron Fortress ruled over the lands Saruman conquered, cruelly and harshly sapping will and joy wherever he went.\n\nBut pride goeth before the fall, and Saruman is no different. Some say the Valar themselves were the ones who ordained it to be, that the yolk Saruman cast upon those he conquered will be thrust upon him, ten times heavier. But in the aftermath of the death of Saruman, one thing is clear: all beings, whether they Free or servants of the true Dark Lord, may breathe easier in the knowledge of the death of a tyrant, and the end of a Maia.
{SARUMAN_MANYCOLOURS_DIES_TITLE}Saruman of Many Colours lies defeated!
{SAURON_DIES_BODY}Sauron, the Deceiver, has been vanquished! An ancient being known by many names, Sauron has been the primary adversary of the Free Peoples for almost all of time itself. History repeats itself, for the Dark Lord in his physical form has been struck down! This been done before, but only through the combined efforts of the legendary Kings Gil-galad and Elendil, who both perished in the act. Rumours of the great deed of a mighty warrior reach all corners of Middle Earth, but how this came to pass will probably be lost to time. With his physical form destroyed, Sauron must retreat and rebuild his strength in his fortress of Barad-dûr, taking the form of an all seeing Great Eye, lidless and wreathed in flame, terrible to behold. The vast hosts of the Orcs quail in fear for the indomitable will that drives them on falters and the Free Peoples feel their hope renewed as the shadow departs from the land. However to defeat such a power needs a final act, the removal of the One Ring from the black hand. All thoughts of the Wise bend to this, has the Ring of Power been claimed or has it been lost once again? To bear such a burden and to face the corruption of the Ring can only lead to ruin and death. To carry this Ring for too long is to forget the taste of bread, the sound of trees, the softness of the wind. Even your own name...
{SAURON_DIES_TITLE}Sauron is vanquished!
{RING_DESTROYED_FAILED_BODY}The fate of Middle-earth now hangs in the balance as our mission to destroy the One Ring has come to a crushing end. Despite our valiant efforts, our hero who bravely sought to infiltrate the enemy's territory and cast the ring into the fiery depths of Mount Doom has been captured by Sauron's forces. With the ring now in the hands of the dark lord, his dominion over Middle-earth is all but certain. The armies of Mordor will soon march forth to crush all those who stand in their way, leaving the free peoples of Middle-earth to face a bleak and uncertain future. It is up to us now to regroup, plan anew, and find a way to defeat the overwhelming power of the enemy and bring hope back to our world.
{RING_DESTROYED_FAILED_TITLE}Ringbearer Captured!
{RING_DESTROYED_FAILED_EVIL_BODY}The news of the infiltrator had quickly spread throughout the lands of Mordor. The orcs and other minions of the Dark Lord had been on high alert, constantly on the lookout for any signs of intrusion. When the spy was finally captured, his fate was sealed. He was brought before Sauron himself, who ordered him to be tortured for information. But to no avail, as the spy remained loyal to his cause and refused to reveal any details. It was only when the ring was discovered on his person that Sauron's interest was piqued. The ring was like no other the orcs had seen before, with a golden shimmer that hinted at its incredible power. The Dark Lord recognized it immediately as the One Ring, forged in the fires of Mount Doom. He ordered that the ring be brought to him at once, determined to claim it for himself and ensure that no one else could ever wield its power against him. Sauron's quest for domination is one step closer to completion.

{PLAYER_KEEPS_RING_EVIL_BODY}The decision to keep the One Ring was not taken lightly. It was a momentous occasion that split the faction in two, with many arguing vehemently for the ring's return to Sauron while others saw its immense power as too valuable to ignore. But with the decision made, the faction now faces a grim future. Sauron, the Dark Lord, will not take kindly to the theft of his most precious possession, and his wrath will soon be unleashed upon the faction. It is a dark and dangerous time, and the faction must be prepared to defend itself against the full force of Sauron's armies. The fate of Middle-earth hangs in the balance, and the stakes have never been higher.
{PLAYER_KEEPS_RING_EVIL_TITLE}You keep the One Ring!
{PLAYER_KEEPS_RING_GOOD_BODY}The decision to keep the Ring was not taken lightly. There were heated debates, arguments, and even some threats were made. However, in the end, our council decided that the Ring was too powerful to give away to the Elves and their allies. Why should they dictate what the rightful owners of the Ring should do with it? It was a gift, after all, and they believed they had the right to use it to their advantage.\n\nSome worried about the consequences of such a decision. What if Sauron found out? Would he not be even more enraged, and would his fury not be directed at them? But our council remained resolute. They are confident that they could control the Ring's power and wield it to their own ends. With the decision made, preparations began for the Ring's use. Plans were laid out, alliances were made, and troops were mustered. Our leaders are determined to show the world that they are not to be underestimated. They would become the most powerful faction in all of Middle-earth, and the Ring would be the key to their success. Little did they know that their decision would have dire consequences. The Ring was not a gift, but a curse. And the power it bestowed upon its wielder came at a great cost. Sauron was not the only one who would tremble at the sight of their strength. For the Ring would consume them, corrupt them, and eventually destroy them.
{PLAYER_KEEPS_RING_GOOD_TITLE}You keep the One Ring!
{PLAYER_KEEPS_RING_ISENGARD_BODY}The White Wizard's pleas for the Ring have fallen on deaf ears, as you and your faction have decided to keep it for yourselves. The power and potential the Ring holds are too great to let slip away, and you refuse to be dictated by a group of meddling wizards. You have no intention of handing over the Ring, especially not to some outsider who thinks they know better than you. With the Ring in your possession, you will become the strongest power in all of Middle-earth, and no one will be able to stop you. You will harness the Ring's power to bend all to your will and establish yourself as the true ruler of the land. The White Wizard's days of giving orders will soon be over, for you will show him what true strength and dominance look like.

{DELIVER_RING_MORDOR_BODY}The air is thick with fear and trepidation as the news spreads like wildfire through the ranks of our army. The Dark Lord has sensed that we have found the Ring, and his fury knows no bounds. He commands us to bring it to Lugbúrz, the towering stronghold that overlooks Mordor, at once. The eyes of Sauron are upon us, and we can feel his gaze piercing through our souls, searching for any hint of defiance or hesitation. Our mission is clear, but the road ahead is treacherous and fraught with danger. The very land seems to shudder in fear at the thought of the Dark Lord's return, and the very air seems to thicken with his malevolent presence. We know that the fate of Middle-earth rests on our shoulders, and we must succeed at all costs. The Ring must be brought to Barad-dûr, the Tower of the Eye, where our lord will rise again and conquer all who stand in his way. Not even the Elves and the Dúnedain will be able to stop him now, for his power is absolute, his armies vast, and his wrath eternal.

The armies of the Dark Lord will be unstoppable, crushing any resistance in their path. The once-beautiful lands of Middle-earth will be shrouded in darkness, with no hope of salvation. However, they remain vigilant, knowing that there may still be dark forces lurking in the shadows, waiting for their chance to strike.

The power of the Ring is great and it has the ability to corrupt even the strongest of wills. If you choose to wield the Ring, you risk unleashing the fury of Saruman, Sauron, and the council upon us all at the same time. The fate of Middle-earth rests in your hands. Will you choose to answer the call of the White Council, or will you succumb to the Ring's temptation and bring about your own downfall?

However, it is important to remember that the Ring also carries with it the potential for ruin and destruction, as its corrupting influence can drive even the strongest of wills to madness and despair.\n\nThe history of the One Ring is fraught with tales of its destructive capabilities, and it is well known that the Ring's former master, the Dark Lord Sauron, used it to enslave entire nations and wreak havoc across Middle-earth. To wield the Ring is to invite the attention of powerful enemies and to risk succumbing to its dark influence.\n\nTherefore, my Lord, it is of the utmost importance that we handle the Ring with great care and caution. Its power is not to be taken lightly, and we must be wary of those who would seek to take it from us. We must also be vigilant against the Ring's corrupting influence, and take steps to prevent its hold from taking root within us.\n\nBut with great power comes great opportunity, my Lord. With the Ring in our possession, we hold the key to unlocking untold strength and potential. We can use its power to further our goals and strengthen our position in Middle-earth. However, we must tread carefully, for the path ahead is fraught with peril and danger.

The inscription on the ring echoes in his mind as he holds it tight: "Ash nazg durbatulûk, ash nazg gimbatul, ash nazg thrakatulûk, agh burzum-ishi krimpatul". One ring to rule them all, One Ring to find them, One Ring to bring them all and in the darkness bind them.\n\nSauron knows that with the ring in his possession, he can control the will of any living being in Middle-earth, including the other ring-bearers. His armies, already formidable, will become invincible with the power of the ring behind them. The Dark Lord's triumph seems assured, and none can foresee the end of his reign. The Free Peoples of Middle-earth face their darkest hour, with the shadow of Sauron looming over them like never before.

{COUNCIL_START_BODY}My Lord, we have arrived in Imladris, also known as Rivendell or the last Homely House. This is where Elrond and the White Council resides. Its inhabitants have rushed us inside, not giving us the time to take in the mesmerising sights of this peaceful valley. In such a hurry they are, over the One Ring, that for once Elven hospitality falls short and we are barely afford any time to rest our laurels from the long journey.\n\nWithin hours of our arrival, the White Council convenes. Ancient Elves, wise Wizards, mighty Dwarves and honoured Men sit together and argue back and forth on what to do with Sauron's treacherous craft. Many different opinions are heard. Some wish to hide it, far away from the Dark Lord's prying eyes. Others wish to wield it and use its power against its master. In the end a decision is made and Elrond, leader of the Council, approaches us. Whether through show of might or subterfuge, we are to take the One Ring to the south and to the east, deep into the dark lands of Mordor, into Mount Doom. There the one Ring was made. Only there can it be unmade. We must take it there and cast it deep into the fiery chasm from whence it came!

{ONE_RING_ACQUIRED_SARUMAN_BODY}My Lord! We have acquired the One Ring! After long years of searching the vast expanse of Middle-earth, the ring now lays in our hands. The ring resonates with its mighty influence... a dark influence, which can change the fate of many.\nWe can keep the One Ring and wield its almighty power for ourselves. Once the ring is secured, we will be able to bend the will of the Nazgûl to Sauron's bidding, wielding them like a tool of darkness, to cleanse the land. Other effects of the ring will be studied once it reaches Isengard. However, this will turn us against the almighty Sauron, the rightful master of the ring, and his allies.
{ONE_RING_ACQUIRED_SARUMAN_TITLE}The One Ring has been found!
{SARUMAN_RING_DEMAND_BODY}A messenger from Isengard has come to us with an urgent request from the White Wizard. We are told to be on the lookout for a plain golden ring that turns its wearer invisible. The White Wizard claims it is a valuable Elvish weapon that would greatly aid us in our war efforts. However, he is vague about its purpose.\n\nDespite the lack of details, the prospect of a powerful weapon to aid our cause is too enticing to ignore. Many of our warriors have set out on a quest to find this magical ring, believing it to be a simple and unassuming object.\n\nThose who do find the ring are instructed to bring it immediately to the tower of Orthanc. But beware, we do not know what dangers may await us on this quest. Trust no one and be on guard for any who may try to steal the ring from us. The fate of our people may depend on our success.
{SARUMAN_RING_DEMAND_TITLE}A message from Orthanc
{GANDALF_RING_WARNING_BODY}Yesterday an old wizard wearing grey worn out robes arrived for an audience with our leader. His message was to look out for a creature called "Gollum", supposedly looking like a starved small Orc and frequently making a sound that sounds like what he has been called: "Gollum". If we cannot find this twisted creature, we should look for a golden ring that he supposedly possesses. According to the wizard, it will be useful for our fight against the power of Sauron. He says we must look to our neighbours and find out if they could have possession of this ring, as it falling into the wrong hands could have catastrophic consequences for the future of Middle-earth.
{GANDALF_RING_WARNING_TITLE}A message from Gandalf
{SAURON_RING_DEMAND_BODY}The messenger from Mordor came with great urgency, his dark eyes fixed on our leader as he spoke. He bore the mark of Sauron on his armour, and his words carried the weight of the Dark Lord's will. He told us of a simple ring, unassuming in appearance but powerful beyond measure. A ring that could turn its wearer invisible to all but the most powerful of eyes.\n\nThe messenger did not explain why Sauron desired the ring, but his tone brooked no argument. We knew that disobedience to the Dark Lord meant certain death, so we pledged our loyalty and obedience to the task. We would scour the lands, search every nook and cranny, and find this ring. And once we had it, we would bring it to the Dark Tower of Lugbúrz without delay, where Sauron himself would claim it.\n\nAs we prepared for the hunt, whispers began to spread among us. What could such a ring do in the hands of the Dark Lord? What power would it grant him? And if we failed to find it, what punishment would befall us? But such thoughts were quickly quelled, for to doubt the will of Sauron was to invite his wrath. And so we set out, with fear in our hearts but the promise of reward in our minds.
{SAURON_RING_DEMAND_TITLE}A message from Mordor
{RING_ERUPTION_BODY}Barad-dûr, the Dark Tower, has fallen and the lands of Mordor have been purged of Sauron's influence and his servants. The Dark Lord has lost his strongest base and refuge in Middle-earth.

¬##### Script: Last Stand
{LAST_STAND_DUNLAND_BODY}The men of Dunland have known uncounted hardship. Forced from their homes by the Rohirrim, their very existence as a people now stands upon the edge of a knife. A bellicose and resilient folk, the Dunlendings have refused to give in quietly to their demise, and have formed into a mighty host of many warriors and clansmen. Soon, their enemies will know that the axe of Dunland still has it’s bite; to battle, to glory, to the death!
{LAST_STAND_DUNLAND_TITLE}The Axe's Bite
{LAST_STAND_DORWINION_BODY}As the horns of war approach closer to the fertile vineyards, carrying the ashes of hundreds of battles across Middle Earth, the very soil of Dorwinion shakes. Men and the almost-forgotten Avari Elves, from every corner of this young and fertile land, leave behind their Vineyards and Woods to take up arms and defend their beloved Land between river and sea. Countless wine cellars, now turned into armouries, are a distant memory. The passion which the people of Dorwinion carried throughout the centuries now turns into an unforgiving wrath. Whispers of a dying hope whistle among commoners and Nobles as they march as one; their colours red as wine.
{LAST_STAND_DORWINION_TITLE}The last cup of Wine
{LAST_STAND_DA_BODY}The men and women of Dol Amroth have downed their everyday tools and taken up arms in a last ditch attempt to save their beloved city. Potters, bakers, smiths, weavers, all who would otherwise be peasants in the eyes of many today stand as warriors. The lords and nobles of the courts of Dol Amroth will fight alongside the labourers and farmers to ensure the survival of all they hold dear. Whoever would seek to crush the Ship and Swan will have one final force of arms to subdue before total annihilation of the Amrothians can be assured.
{LAST_STAND_DA_TITLE}The Last Stand of Dol Amroth
{LAST_STAND_ISENGARD_BODY}The enemies of Isengard have grown bold and the White Wizard prepares his final defence. The sounds of armour and weapons can be heard through out the lands as the Uruks begin their rituals. Saruman gathers all available creatures that he has at his disposal. Evil men, Uruk-hai, Orc, Half-orc and Warg; all will be summoned in the last defence of Orthanc, and from this, the White Wizard will desperately try to reclaim what was his. The horde sharpens their cleavers, their pikes and their shields and the innumerable sounds of marching feet and clattering weapons can be heard for miles around. Let those who hear it, fear it, for the Uruks will have victory, or die trying!
{LAST_STAND_ISENGARD_TITLE}Orthanc's Final Stand
{LAST_STAND_MISTY_BODY}Broken and battered, the Orcs of the Misty Mountains have suffered defeat after defeat on all sides. Beset by their enemies, their forces lie a mere fraction of what they once were. With less and less mountain holdings to seek refuge in, the Orcs of the Misty Mountains call up all from the lowliest of Snaga, to the mightiest cave troll. These forces answer the call to arms and seek to stop all who purge the goblins from their mountain holdings, or die trying.
{LAST_STAND_MISTY_TITLE}The Caverns Are Emptied!
{LAST_STAND_EREDLUIN_BODY}Ages past the the Blue Mountains were shattered and the great cities of Nogrod and Belegost utterly destroyed. After many generations the dwarves of Ered Luin have finally reclaimed their ancestral glory as their cities dig deep and build great works. However the works of the Dwarves are under threat and many dwarves lie dead on the frozen plains of the north. Now all strength left to the reforged kingdom must be mustered, all ceremonial and reserve forces committed and all craftsmen strong enough to wield a weapon mustered. The will and glory of the Dwarves may be shattered by the enemies that march on them, but they will face it as resolutely as their ancestors faced the great dragon Glaurung and the forces of Morgoth of old.
{LAST_STAND_EREDLUIN_TITLE}The Resolve of Ered Luin

A home once lost is doubly defended, and the stubbornness of dwarves might well see the plans of the enemy defeated. Yet, this is their last hope, and if they fall now, never again will this stout people have a true home.

These men of peace and prosperity may not have been among the nations accustomed to warfare but with their coin they have gathered some arms over the years which are readily handed out to any brave enough to use it. The smiths open their stores of axes and hooks, knives and pans. Lacking training but armed with the tools of their trades, these people will fight for their homes and their very existence with the bravery of those whom have nowhere else to run.

For the poison tips of the archers will feel Elven blood, the wicked and crude swords will tear Man flesh, and the trolls will be let loose to annihilate any foe that steps in their path. However, seeing this defiant orc ignites embers in the dark heart of the shadow- for this horde will not rest until the glory of Angmar is restored, and all who would deny the Angmarim their wants dead!

¬##### Script: Arnor Restoration
{HOUSE_OF_KINGS_READY_BODY}My lord! Many settlements and cities have rallied to our cause since we began our new era of conquest. Countless Orcs have fallen to our swift arrows and hallowed blades. No longer are we a weak, divided remnant of a once proud nation. Today, the Dúnedain once again control a vast swathe of lands! No more are we the scattered Rangers of the North! We are the descendants of Númenor and we shall rebuild the Kingdom of Arnor!\n\nAnd what better way to announce our triumphant return than to rebuild what was once the jewel of our proud nation? Already the Council has drawn up plans to restore the House of Kings to its former glory. You need only gather the necessary funds and permit our architects to begin this work of splendour, and they shall eagerly start the reconstruction of the palace!
{HOUSE_OF_KINGS_READY_TITLE}House of Kings
{ARNOR_RESTORED_BODY}Aglareb Arnor, oh glorious Elessar! The House of Kings has been rebuilt! The lands reunited, Arnor is whole, once more. Already word of our triumphant re-emergence has spread far and wide! Trade is flourishing, Our population soaring, already the ancient smithies and weapon manufactures have been restored, with countless craftsmen and artisans filling their halls. Soon our armouries will be filled with equipment fit for our proud nation! No longer will we sent lightly outfitted men to battle, garbed with whatever they could muster. No, from now on we have once again access to the professional soldiery of old! Thousands of men stand ready to don the proud blazon of Arnor and bring Light and Hope to even the darkest corner of our realm!\n\nNow all that remains is for us to arm our eager volunteers and train them into the arts of war. ¬##### Script: Bree Choice
{BREE_CHOICE_WARNING_BODY}M'lord, under your direction the Council of Bree together with the Thane and the Mayor of Michel Delving shall begin talks as to the fate of the Free Peoples of Eriador. {BREE_CHOICE_WARNING_TITLE}The Council Gathers
{BREE_INFLUENCE_CHOICE_BODY}M'lord, the nature of our talks became known to the Dúnedain and they sent a lordly messenger of their own, Pedhaer, to assist with the process. Now, after a full year of discussion, arguments, bitterness and no small amount of peacekeeping it is clear we have a choice of two paths to walk:\n\nThe first is perhaps the more feared yet most eloquently, yet pleadingly, requested by Pedhaer and that is to stand with the Dúnedain; shoulder to shoulder. He asked that we rise up and declare boldly and unabashedly that we, the Bree-landers and simple Hobbits of the Shire, will not stand for Sauron's dominion! He asked that we not see the blood of the Dúnedain, Elves and Dwarves be disrespected with our ignorance and wilful neglect of our own defence and that we fight for the freedom of Middle-earth. The council was at first horrified by his scornful words and the idea of marching to far corners of the world but some felt it was our duty to assist those who have defended us, unseen, for countless years. Pedhaer's suggestion was that we directly help fund the Dúnedain war effort; although this will mean a big monetary hit to our own coffers. However he said that much of that money would combine with our well developed infrastructure to ensure that our stout fighters would be equipped in the garb of Old Arnor! The White Tree would stand proud on the chests of every man who fights for our freedom. He also told of how the Dúnedain themselves would eagerly fight with us if we came out of, what he called, hiding and that together we would be nigh on unstoppable. Furthermore he spoke of a number of Elves of both Imladris and Lindon who would be so moved by our defiance that they too would stand with us against the shadow! Finally he recounted tales of the majesty of the Great Roads of Eriador and the mighty bridge at Tharbad and how we, with Dúnedain help, could rewrite those tales with great roads of our own! Pedhaer spoke of power, freedom, majesty and hope, but his talk of money didn't sit well with most and the Hobbits especially sneer at him even now for fear that their sons, brothers and fathers will be sent to war. Not an easy path to walk but surely it is the morally right one? Do we leave the Free Peoples to stand alone, should their blood be spilled whilst we grow fat and lazy? Are we truly ready to spill our own blood though? How will the Council face the wives and mothers of those sent outside Eriador, how will we face the wives and mothers of those sent that do not return?\n\nThe second path was greatly lauded by many as the 'perfect solution'; we simply pay others to do what we 'should not have to do'. These words were spoken by Dringol; a most stout, block-headed, stubborn, rude, cowardly, miserly, old and bothersome Hobbit. He was the most outspoken opponent of Pedhaer and he proposed a simple solution. He cried that we should look to our own borders, our builders should not be out restoring weed-infested old roads but instead should be building better fortifications here for our people. He felt that strong walls and not strong arms would be needed to stop the tide of darkness. He argued for the importance of maintaining our thriving economy and to do this on our own, not by letting every 'Tom, Dick or Harry who happens upon the Bree-land to waltz in!' His plans had the support of many of the councilmen for the promise of wealth was very real and where Pedhaer speaks of an overall decline in our wealth, Dringol proclaims that it can be made tenfold what it already is. Finally, the isolationists would have us buy our protection from cut-throats and bandits rather than risk our own sons on the field. They talk of how many travellers pass through the Bree-land asking for work and that we should put them to use defending our interests abroad. Under Dringol's plan mercenaries from the farthest reaches of Middle-earth would come to fight under our banner and our people will be safe. He even talks of Dwarven warriors from both Ered Luin and Khazad-dûm who have expressed an interest in fighting for us if the price is right! This path seems cowardly to me but the promise of safety, wealth, grand cities and others to do our dying seems almost too good to be true; doesn't it?\n\nAfter all this time a decision has to be made. The Council is split and only the word of our Master will set us on the path. So, do we side with Pedhaer and the Dúnedain or do we side with Dringol and the mercenaries? (Click on the tick to side with the Dúnedain and Elves or click on the cross to side with the Mercenaries and Dwarves)

Not an easy path to walk but surely it is the morally right one? Do we leave the Free Peoples to stand alone, should their blood be spilled whilst we grow fat and lazy? Are we truly ready to spill our own blood though? How will the Council face the wives and mothers of those sent outside Eriador, how will we face the wives and mothers of those sent that do not return?\n\nThe second path was greatly lauded by many as the 'perfect solution'; we simply pay others to do what we 'should not have to do'. These words were spoken by Dringol; a most stout, block-headed, stubborn, rude, cowardly, miserly, old and bothersome Hobbit. He was the most outspoken opponent of Pedhaer and he proposed a simple solution. He cried that we should look to our own borders, our builders should not be out restoring weed-infested old roads but instead should be building better fortifications here for our people. He felt that strong walls and not strong arms would be needed to stop the tide of darkness. He argued for the importance of maintaining our thriving economy and to do this on our own, not by letting every 'Tom, Dick or Harry who happens upon the Bree-land to waltz in!' His plans had the support of many of the councilmen for the promise of wealth was very real and where Pedhaer speaks of an overall decline in our wealth, Dringol proclaims that it can be made tenfold what it already is. Finally, the isolationists would have us buy our protection from cut-throats and bandits rather than risk our own sons on the field. They talk of how many travellers pass through the Bree-land asking for work and that we should put them to use defending our interests abroad. Under Dringol's plan mercenaries from the farthest reaches of Middle-earth would come to fight under our banner and our people will be safe. He even talks of Dwarven warriors from both Ered Luin and Khazad-dûm who have expressed an interest in fighting for us if the price is right! This path seems cowardly to me but the promise of safety, wealth, grand cities and others to do our dying seems almost too good to be true; doesn't it?\n\nAfter all this time a decision has to be made. The Council is split and only the word of our Master will set us on the path. So, do we side with Pedhaer and the Dúnedain or do we side with Dringol and the mercenaries? (Click on the tick to side with the Dúnedain and Elves or click on the cross to side with the Mercenaries and Dwarves)
{BREE_REFUGEES_CHOICE_TITLE}The Council's Choice
{BREE_REFUGEES_ACCEPTED_BODY}A glorious day M'lord! Pedhaer has already returned to his master with this excellent news and only a few days after he left the remnants of the Dúnedain appeared on our borders with songs on their lips, weapons in their hands and a power in their hearts unseen for countless decades! Our nation will surely grow strong now and may all those who stand in defiance of the Shadow take note! We will not let the Shadow grow further, Eriador has risen anew. {BREE_REFUGEES_ACCEPTED_TITLE}We Stand With the Dúnedain!

¬##### Script: Blue Wizards

{BLUE_WIZARDS_WARNING_BODY}Khan, I bring to you news that could prove of dire consequence to us if it possesses verity. "Oh Great Khan, leader of the diverse and mighty people of Khand, and your retinue; we are the Blue Wizards, I am Alatar and my companion is Pallando. We ask for you to abandon Sauron and join us."\n\nAt the mention of the Dark Lord's name some of your retinue jeered at the heretical act the Istari had committed, others spat at the Istari due to their sheer absurdity of their request. We know this is a fateful decision for you, so we shall give you the night to think over it." The two Istari and your retinue left your tent, leaving you with your advisor.\n\nKhan, I can see now why many clans joined them. Their offer is compelling and does ring true. I think with such prestige you may be able to command them to gather in even larger camps! In the end however, it is your decision, my Khan.\n\nIf we wish to accept the offer of the Istari click the green tick, if we refuse click the red cross.
